definition{
  name: runWarm,
  parameters: [state : Bytes, input : Bytes, profile : Nat],
  result: Bytes,
  body: (if ((prim length input : Nat) <? 4294967296) then (let n : Nat := (prim length input : Nat); (let cap : Nat := ((prim multiply 2 n : Nat) + 64); (let w : Nat := (prim length state : Nat); (finish (encodeWarm (start state input (w + n) cap profile) n cap w profile) n)))) else 0x),
  executable: true,

}

  parameters: [input : Bytes],
  result: Bytes,

  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}
